            <option value="in_progress" ${state.mastery[q.id]==='in_progress'?'selected':''}>Status: In Progress</option>
            <option value="needs_revision" ${state.mastery[q.id]==='needs_revision'?'selected':''}>Status: Needs Revision</option>
            <option value="mastered" ${state.mastery[q.id]==='mastered'?'selected':''}>Status: Mastered ✓</option>
          </select>
          ${prevQ ? `<button class="btn" onclick="selectQuestion('${prevQ.id}')">← Q${prevQ.number}</button>` : ''}
          ${nextQ ? `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="selectQuestion('${nextQ.id}')">Q${nextQ.number} →</button>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="phase-tabs" role="tablist">
      ${tabs.map(t => `
        <button class="phase-tab ${state.currentTab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="switchPhaseTab('${t.id}')">
          ${escapeHtml(t.label)}
        </button>
      `).join('')}
    </div>

    <div id="tab-content-area">
      ${renderActiveTabContent(q)}
    </div>
  `;
}

function renderActiveTabContent(q) {
  switch (state.currentTab) {
    case 'classroom': return renderClassroomTab(q);
    case 'decoder': return renderDecoderTab(q);
    case 'viz': return renderVizTab(q);
    case 'solution': return renderSolutionTab(q);
    case 'exam': return renderExamTab(q);
    case 'memory': return renderMemoryTab(q);
    case 'practice': return renderPracticeTab(q);
    default: return renderClassroomTab(q);
  }
}

// --- TAB 1: CONCEPT CLASSROOM (PHASE 2 & PHASE 10) ---
function renderClassroomTab(q) {
  const c = q.classroom;
  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🏛️ Phase 2: Prerequisite Concept Classroom — Taught Before Solving Q${q.number}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchPhaseTab('decoder')">Next: Question Decoder →</button>
      </div>
      <div style="margin-bottom:0.85rem;">
        <strong>Prerequisites for Q${q.number}:</strong>
        <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.4rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">
          ${q.prerequisites.map(p => `<span class="badge badge-blue">${escapeHtml(p)}</span>`).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="box-assumption">
          <h4>A. Motivation: Why Does This Concept Exist?</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">${escapeHtml(c.motivation)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-transform">
          <h4>B. Intuitive & Geometric Explanation</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">${escapeHtml(c.intuition)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin:1rem 0 0.5rem;">C. Rigorous Mathematical Definitions & Symbol Glossary</h4>
      ${c.formalDefinitions.map(d => `
        <div class="box-step">
          <div style="font-weight:700; color:var(--color-step);">${escapeHtml(d.term)}</div>
          <div class="math-block">${escapeHtml(d.formula)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:0.84rem; color:var(--text-secondary);"><strong>Symbol Breakdown:</strong> ${escapeHtml(d.symbols)}</div>
        </div>
      `).join('')}

      <h4 style="margin:1rem 0 0.5rem;">D. Examples & Crucial Counterexample</h4>
      <div class="grid-3">
        <div class="box-conclusion">
          <strong>1. Simple Example</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.87rem; margin-top:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(c.examples.simple)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-assumption">
          <strong>2. Nontrivial Example</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.87rem; margin-top:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(c.examples.nontrivial)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-danger">
          <strong>3. Counterexample (Why Assumptions Matter!)</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.87rem; margin-top:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(c.examples.counterexample)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2" style="margin-top:0.9rem;">
        <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;">
          <div class="card-title">🔗 E. Connection to Earlier Concepts</div>
          <p style="font-size:0.89rem;">${escapeHtml(c.connectionToPrior)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0; border-left:4px solid var(--accent-secondary);">
          <div class="card-title">🤖 F. AI, Machine Learning & Optimization Connection</div>
          <p style="font-size:0.89rem;">${escapeHtml(c.aiConnections)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>✅ G. Adaptive Prerequisite Checkpoints (Phase 2G & Phase 10 Tutor)</span>
        <span class="badge badge-purple">Verify understanding before viewing proof</span>
      </div>
      ${c.checkpoints.map((cp, idx) => {
        const key = `${q.id}_cp_${idx}`;
        const chosen = state.checkpointAnswers[key];
        const isAnswered = chosen !== undefined;
        const isRight = chosen === cp.correctIndex;
        return `
          <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated); margin-bottom:0.85rem;">
            <div style="font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.45rem;">Checkpoint ${idx + 1}: ${escapeHtml(cp.question)}</div>
            ${cp.options.map((opt, oIdx) => {
              let cls = 'quiz-option';
              if (isAnswered) {
                if (oIdx === cp.correctIndex) cls += ' correct';
                else if (oIdx === chosen) cls += ' wrong';
              }
              return `<button class="${cls}" onclick="answerCheckpoint('${q.id}', ${idx}, ${oIdx})">${String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)}. ${escapeHtml(opt)}</button>`;
            }).join('')}
            <div style="margin-top:0.45rem;">
              <button class="btn" style="font-size:0.78rem; padding:0.25rem 0.6rem;" onclick="alert('Hint: ' + ${JSON.stringify(cp.hint)})">💡 Show Hint</button>
            </div>
            ${isAnswered ? `
              <div class="${isRight ? 'box-conclusion' : 'box-danger'}" style="margin-top:0.65rem; margin-bottom:0;">
                <strong>${isRight ? '✓ Correct Reasoning!' : '✗ Misconception Diagnosis:'}</strong>
                <p style="font-size:0.87rem; margin-top:0.2rem;">${escapeHtml(cp.misconceptions[chosen])}</p>
                <p style="font-size:0.84rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-weight:600;">🎯 Follow-Up Insight: ${escapeHtml(cp.followUp)}</p>
              </div>
            ` : ''}
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
}

function answerCheckpoint(qId, cpIdx, optIdx) {
  state.checkpointAnswers[`${qId}_cp_${cpIdx}`] = optIdx;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

// --- TAB 2: QUESTION DECODER (PHASE 3) ---
function renderDecoderTab(q) {
  const d = q.decoder;
  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🔍 Phase 3: Exam Question Decoder — Q${q.number}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchPhaseTab('viz')">Next: Interactive Visualization Lab →</button>
      </div>

      <div class="box-assumption">
        <h4>1. Original Question & Instructor Hint (Verbatim from Problem Sheet 6)</h4>
        <div class="math-block" style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(q.originalText)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:0.88rem; color:var(--text-secondary); margin-top:0.35rem;"><strong>Official Sheet Hint:</strong> ${escapeHtml(q.originalHint)}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="box-assumption">
          <h4>2. Given Information (Hypotheses & Spaces)</h4>
          <ul style="padding-left:1.2rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.9rem;">
            ${d.given.map(g => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(g)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
        <div class="box-conclusion">
          <h4>3. Exact Mathematical Objective</h4>
          <ul style="padding-left:1.2rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.9rem;">
            ${d.objective.map(o => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(o)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin:0.9rem 0 0.45rem;">4. Hidden Mathematical Clues & Theorem Triggers</h4>
      <table class="data-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Wording / Clue in Question</th>
            <th>Hidden Mathematical Meaning</th>
            <th>Candidate Theorem / Technique Triggered</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          ${d.clues.map(c => `
            <tr>
              <td><span class="badge badge-amber">${escapeHtml(c.keyword)}</span></td>
              <td>${escapeHtml(c.meaning)}</td>
              <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.theoremTriggered)}</strong></td>
            </tr>
          `).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      <div class="grid-2" style="margin-top:0.95rem;">
        <div class="box-transform">
          <h4>5. First 20–30 Seconds Thought Process in the Exam</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">${escapeHtml(d.first30Seconds)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-danger">
          <h4>6. Common Traps & Invalid Assumptions to Avoid</h4>
          <ul style="padding-left:1.2rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.88rem;">
            ${d.commonTraps.map(t => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(t)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="box-step" style="margin-top:0.5rem;">
        <h4>7. Concise Solution Strategy Roadmap</h4>
        <p style="font-size:0.92rem; margin-top:0.3rem; white-space:pre-line;">${escapeHtml(d.strategy)}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// --- TAB 3: VISUALIZATION LAB (PHASE 4) ---
function renderVizTab(q) {
  const v = q.visualization;
  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🎬 Phase 4: Interactive Mathematical Visualization Lab — ${escapeHtml(v.title)}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchPhaseTab('solution')">Next: Guided Solution & Compare Methods →</button>
      </div>

      <div class="box-transform" style="padding:0.65rem 1rem; font-size:0.85rem;">
        <strong>⚠️ Mathematical Accuracy & Finite-Dimensional Analogy Notice:</strong> ${escapeHtml(v.analogyDisclaimer)}
        <em>Remember: A geometric diagram builds intuition to reconstruct a proof, but never substitutes for a formal proof.</em>
      </div>

      <div class="viz-container">
        <div id="viz-telemetry-bar" style="font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:0.83rem; background:var(--bg-surface); padding:0.55rem 0.85rem; border-radius:6px; border:1px solid var(--border-color); margin-bottom:0.65rem;">
          Initializing mathematical simulation...
        </div>
        <div class="viz-canvas-wrap">
          <canvas id="fa-math-canvas" class="math-canvas" width="900" height="390"></canvas>
        </div>
        <div class="viz-controls" id="viz-controls-bar"></div>
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin:0.9rem 0 0.5rem;">Structured 6-Point Mathematical Visual Guide</h4>
      <div class="grid-3">
        <div class="box-assumption">
          <strong>1. What am I seeing?</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(v.guide.seeing)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-step">
          <strong>2. Mathematical Objects Represented</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(v.guide.objects)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-transform">
          <strong>3. What Changes During Animation?</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(v.guide.changes)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-conclusion">
          <strong>4. What Property Remains Invariant?</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(v.guide.invariant)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-assumption">
          <strong>5. How Does This Help Prove the Result?</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(v.guide.proofHelp)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-conclusion">
          <strong>6. Key Visual Takeaway to Remember</strong>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem; font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(v.guide.takeaway)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// --- TAB 4: GUIDED SOLUTION & PHASE 5A COMPARE SOLUTIONS ---
function renderSolutionTab(q) {
  const s = q.solutions;
  const m = s.methods;
  const revCount = state.revealedSteps[q.id] || 1;
  const totalSteps = s.layer3Steps.length;
  const curMethodKey = state.selectedMethod[q.id] || 'methodB';
  const curMethod = m.details[curMethodKey] || m.details.methodB;
  const compareAll = state.compareAllMode[q.id];

  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🧭 Phase 5: Progressive 5-Layer Guided Solution — Q${q.number}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchPhaseTab('exam')">Next: Full-Marks Exam Answer →</button>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="box-assumption">
          <h4>Layer 1: Intuitive Solution (Core Idea in Ordinary Language)</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">${escapeHtml(s.layer1Intuitive)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-transform">
          <h4>Layer 2: Mathematical Strategy & Theorem Selection</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">${escapeHtml(s.layer2Strategy)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated); margin-top:0.5rem;">
        <div class="card-title">
          <span>Layer 3: Step-by-Step Rigorous Derivation (Progressive Disclosure: ${revCount} / ${totalSteps} Steps Shown)</span>
          <div style="display:flex; gap:0.4rem;">
            ${revCount < totalSteps ? `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="revealNextStep('${q.id}', ${totalSteps})">+ Reveal Next Step (${revCount + 1}/${totalSteps})</button>` : ''}
            <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="setRevealedSteps('${q.id}', ${totalSteps})">Reveal All Steps</button>
            <button class="btn" onclick="setRevealedSteps('${q.id}', 1)">Reset to Step 1</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        ${s.layer3Steps.slice(0, revCount).map((st, i) => `
          <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0.75rem; border-left:4px solid var(--color-step);">
            <div style="font-weight:700; color:var(--color-step); margin-bottom:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(st.title)}</div>
            <div class="math-block">${escapeHtml(st.math)}</div>
            <div class="grid-3" style="font-size:0.83rem; margin-top:0.4rem;">
              <div><strong>Why Valid:</strong> ${escapeHtml(st.whyValid)}</div>
              <div><strong>Theorem / Property:</strong> <span class="badge badge-blue">${escapeHtml(st.theorem)}</span></div>
              <div><strong>Contribution:</strong> ${escapeHtml(st.contribution)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>

      <div class="box-conclusion">
        <h4>Layer 4: Proof Verification & Infinite-Dimensional Audit</h4>
        <ul style="padding-left:1.2rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.88rem;">
          ${s.layer4Verification.map(v => `<li>✓ ${escapeHtml(v)}</li>`).join('')}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--color-transform);">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>⚡ Phase 5A: Discover the Easiest & Smartest Solution ("Compare Solutions" Studio)</span>
        <span class="badge badge-green">Recommended: ${escapeHtml(m.recommended)}</span>
      </div>
      <p style="font-size:0.89rem; margin-bottom:0.75rem;"><strong>Why Recommended:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.whyRecommended)}</p>

      <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.5rem; margin-bottom:0.9rem;">
        <button class="btn ${!compareAll && curMethodKey==='methodA'?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="selectSolutionMethod('${q.id}', 'methodA')">✨ Show Easiest Solution (Method A)</button>
        <button class="btn ${!compareAll && curMethodKey==='methodB'?'btn-success':''}" onclick="selectSolutionMethod('${q.id}', 'methodB')">⚡ Show Shortest Exam Proof (Method B)</button>
        <button class="btn ${!compareAll && curMethodKey==='methodC'?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="selectSolutionMethod('${q.id}', 'methodC')">📘 Standard Textbook Method (Method C)</button>
        <button class="btn ${!compareAll && curMethodKey==='methodD'?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="selectSolutionMethod('${q.id}', 'methodD')">🧠 Clever Alternative Method (Method D)</button>
        <button class="btn ${compareAll?'btn-amber':''}" onclick="toggleCompareAllMethods('${q.id}')">🔀 Side-by-Side All 4 Methods</button>
      </div>

      ${compareAll ? `
        <div class="grid-2">
          ${['methodA', 'methodB', 'methodC', 'methodD'].map(k => renderMethodDetailCard(m.details[k])).join('')}
        </div>
      ` : renderMethodDetailCard(curMethod)}

      <h4 style="margin:1rem 0 0.45rem;">Method Comparison Table</h4>
      <table class="data-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Method</th>
            <th>Difficulty</th>
            <th>Proof Length</th>
            <th>Est. Exam Time</th>
            <th>Easy to Remember?</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          ${m.comparisonTable.map(row => `
            <tr>
              <td><strong>${escapeHtml(row.method)}</strong></td>
              <td>${escapeHtml(row.difficulty)}</td>
              <td>${escapeHtml(row.length)}</td>
              <td>${escapeHtml(row.time)}</td>
              <td><span class="badge ${row.memorable.includes('Yes')?'badge-green':'badge-amber'}">${escapeHtml(row.memorable)}</span></td>
            </tr>
          `).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      <div class="grid-2" style="margin-top:0.95rem;">
        <div class="box-transform">
          <h4>🎯 Shortcut Recognition Guide</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.3rem;"><strong>• Clue in Prompt:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.shortcutGuide.clue)}</p>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;"><strong>• Key Theorem:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.shortcutGuide.theorem)}</p>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;"><strong>• Unnecessary Steps to Avoid:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.shortcutGuide.avoid)}</p>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;"><strong>• Required Assumptions:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.shortcutGuide.assumptions)}</p>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;"><strong>• When Shortcut Fails:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.shortcutGuide.failsWhen)}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="box-conclusion">
          <h4>🧠 Phase 5A Memory Optimization (Recommended Exam Method)</h4>
          <p style="font-size:0.88rem; margin-top:0.3rem;"><strong>One-Line Proof Idea:</strong> ${escapeHtml(m.memoryOptimization.oneLineIdea)}</p>
          <div style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.4rem;"><strong>Three-Step Memory Skeleton:</strong>
            <ul style="padding-left:1.1rem; margin-top:0.2rem;">
              ${m.memoryOptimization.threeStepSkeleton.map(st => `<li>${escapeHtml(st)}</li>`).join('')}
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:0.45rem;"><strong>Trigger Words:</strong>
            ${m.memoryOptimization.triggerWords.map(tw => `<span class="badge badge-green" style="margin-right:0.3rem;">${escapeHtml(tw)}</span>`).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function renderMethodDetailCard(md) {
  return `
    <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated); margin-bottom:0.85rem;">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>${escapeHtml(md.name)}</span>
        <span class="badge badge-green">${escapeHtml(md.Badge)} • ⏱️ ${escapeHtml(md.time)}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:0.8rem; font-weight:700; color:var(--text-muted);">Animated Proof Flowchart:</div>
      <div class="flowchart-row">
        ${md.flowchart.map((node, i) => `
          <span class="flow-node">${escapeHtml(node)}</span>
          ${i < md.flowchart.length - 1 ? '<span class="flow-arrow">→</span>' : ''}
        `).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="math-block" style="margin-top:0.5rem;">${escapeHtml(md.text)}</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function revealNextStep(qId, maxSteps) {
  const cur = state.revealedSteps[qId] || 1;
  if (cur < maxSteps) state.revealedSteps[qId] = cur + 1;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function setRevealedSteps(qId, count) {
  state.revealedSteps[qId] = count;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function selectSolutionMethod(qId, mKey) {
  state.compareAllMode[qId] = false;
  state.selectedMethod[qId] = mKey;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function toggleCompareAllMethods(qId) {
  state.compareAllMode[qId] = !state.compareAllMode[qId];
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

// --- TAB 5: EXAM ANSWER ("WHAT TO WRITE IN THE EXAMINATION" — PHASE 6) ---
function renderExamTab(q) {
  const e = q.examAnswer;
  const mode = state.examMode[q.id] || 'full';
  const proofText = mode === 'full' ? e.fullProof : e.quickRevision;

  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>✍️ Phase 6: "What to Write in the Examination" — Q${q.number}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchPhaseTab('memory')">Next: Proof Memory Palace →</button>
      </div>

      <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; margin-bottom:0.85rem;">
        <div style="display:flex; gap:0.45rem;">
          <button class="btn ${mode==='full'?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="setExamMode('${q.id}', 'full')">📜 Full Proof Mode (Complete Rigorous Solution)</button>
          <button class="btn ${mode==='quick'?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="setExamMode('${q.id}', 'quick')">⚡ Quick Revision Mode (Shortest Sufficient Proof)</button>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; gap:0.45rem;">
          <button class="btn" onclick="copyExamProof()">📋 Copy Answer to Clipboard</button>
          <button class="btn" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ Print Exam Sheet</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="box-conclusion">
        <div style="font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.35rem;">Model Examination Script (${mode === 'full' ? 'Full Proof Mode' : 'Quick Revision Mode'}):</div>
        <div class="math-block" id="current-exam-proof-text" style="background:var(--bg-surface); font-size:1rem;">${escapeHtml(proofText)}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated); margin-top:1rem;">
        <div class="card-title">
          <span>🎓 Examiner's Marking Perspective</span>
          <span class="badge badge-amber">Estimated Breakdown: ${escapeHtml(e.markingPerspective.estimatedTotal)} (Estimate — Not Official Scheme)</span>
        </div>
        <div class="grid-3">
          <div class="box-conclusion">
            <strong>Essential Steps That Earn Marks</strong>
            <ul style="padding-left:1.1rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.86rem;">
              ${e.markingPerspective.essential.map(item => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(item)}</li>`).join('')}
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="box-danger">
            <strong>Omissions That Lose Marks</strong>
            <ul style="padding-left:1.1rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.86rem;">
              ${e.markingPerspective.omissionsToLoseMarks.map(item => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(item)}</li>`).join('')}
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="box-transform">
            <strong>Common Exam Mistakes</strong>
            <ul style="padding-left:1.1rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.86rem;">
              ${e.markingPerspective.commonMistakes.map(item => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(item)}</li>`).join('')}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function setExamMode(qId, mode) {
  state.examMode[qId] = mode;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function copyExamProof() {
  const el = document.getElementById('current-exam-proof-text');
  if (!el) return;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(el.innerText).then(() => {
    alert('Copied Examination Answer to clipboard!');
  }).catch(() => {
    alert('Select and copy the text from the box.');
  });
}

// --- TAB 6: MEMORY PALACE (PHASE 7) ---
function renderMemoryTab(q) {
  const mp = q.memoryPalace;
  const hintLvl = state.recallHintLevel[q.id] || 0;
  const errChosen = state.errorDetectionAnswers ? state.errorDetectionAnswers[q.id] : state.errorDetAnswers[q.id];
  const errAnswered = errChosen !== undefined;

  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🧠 Phase 7: Permanent Proof Memory System — Q${q.number}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchPhaseTab('practice')">Next: Unseen Practice Arena →</button>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="box-step">
          <h4>A. Compressed Proof Skeleton (Logical Backbone)</h4>
          <ul style="padding-left:1.15rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.89rem;">
            ${mp.skeleton.map(s => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.28rem;">${escapeHtml(s)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
        <div class="box-transform">
          <h4>B & C. Memory Anchors & Story / Spatial Encoding</h4>
          ${mp.anchors.map(a => `<p style="font-size:0.88rem; margin-top:0.25rem; font-weight:600;">⚓ ${escapeHtml(a)}</p>`).join('')}
          <p style="font-size:0.87rem; margin-top:0.5rem; border-top:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-top:0.4rem;"><strong>📖 Story Metaphor:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.story)}</p>
          <p style="font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:0.4rem; color:var(--accent-secondary);"><strong>🤖 J. Personal AI/ML Hook:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.aiHook)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated);">
        <h4>D. Visual Memory Map: Assumptions → Theorem → Argument → Conclusion</h4>
        <div class="flowchart-row" style="margin-top:0.55rem;">
          <div class="flow-node" style="border-color:var(--color-assumption);"><strong>Given:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.visualMap.given)}</div>
          <span class="flow-arrow">⟹</span>
          <div class="flow-node" style="border-color:var(--color-transform);"><strong>Key Theorem:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.visualMap.theorem)}</div>
          <span class="flow-arrow">⟹</span>
          <div class="flow-node" style="border-color:var(--color-step);"><strong>Critical Argument:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.visualMap.argument)}</div>
          <span class="flow-arrow">⟹</span>
          <div class="flow-node" style="border-color:var(--color-conclusion);"><strong>Conclusion:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.visualMap.conclusion)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated);">
          <div class="card-title">
            <span>🙈 E. Recall Without Looking (Active Reconstruction)</span>
            <span class="badge badge-purple">Hints Used: ${hintLvl} / ${mp.recallHints.length}</span>
          </div>
          <p style="font-size:0.84rem; color:var(--text-secondary);">Type or outline the proof from memory below before revealing progressive hints:</p>
          <textarea class="scratchpad" placeholder="Reconstruct the proof of Q${q.number} from memory here..."></textarea>
          <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.4rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">
            ${hintLvl < mp.recallHints.length ? `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="stepRecallHint('${q.id}', 1)">💡 Reveal Hint ${hintLvl + 1}</button>` : ''}
            <button class="btn" onclick="stepRecallHint('${q.id}', 0)">Reset Hints</button>
            <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="setMastery('${q.id}', 'mastered')">✓ I Reconstructed It (Mark Mastered)</button>
            <button class="btn btn-amber" onclick="setMastery('${q.id}', 'needs_revision')">⚠️ Flag for Revision</button>
          </div>
          ${hintLvl > 0 ? `
            <div class="box-assumption" style="margin-top:0.65rem; margin-bottom:0;">
              ${mp.recallHints.slice(0, hintLvl).map(h => `<div style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-bottom:0.25rem;">• ${escapeHtml(h)}</div>`).join('')}
            </div>
          ` : ''}
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated);">
          <div class="card-title">
            <span>🧩 F. Fill-in-the-Proof Exercise</span>
            ${state.fillBlankStatus[q.id] ? '<span class="badge badge-green">✓ Verified Correct</span>' : ''}
          </div>
          <p style="font-size:0.84rem; color:var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom:0.5rem;">${escapeHtml(mp.fillInBlanks.prompt)}</p>
          ${mp.fillInBlanks.items.map((item, idx) => `
            <div style="margin-bottom:0.65rem; font-size:0.88rem;">
              <div>${idx + 1}. ${escapeHtml(item.sentence)}</div>
              <select class="util-select" id="fill-${q.id}-${idx}" style="margin-top:0.25rem;">
                <option value="">-- Select missing mathematical phrase --</option>
                ${item.options.map(opt => `<option value="${escapeHtml(opt)}">${escapeHtml(opt)}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
          `).join('')}
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="checkFillBlanks('${q.id}')">Check Fill-in-the-Proof</button>
          <div id="fill-feedback-${q.id}" style="margin-top:0.5rem; font-size:0.86rem;"></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated);">
        <div class="card-title">
          <span>🕵️ G. Error Detection Exercise ("Spot the Fatal Mathematical Flaw")</span>
        </div>
        <div class="box-danger">
          <strong>Plausible But Incorrect Student Proof:</strong>
          <div class="math-block" style="background:var(--bg-surface);">${escapeHtml(mp.errorDetection.flawedProof)}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.4rem;">${escapeHtml(mp.errorDetection.question)}</div>
        ${mp.errorDetection.options.map((opt, oIdx) => {
          let cls = 'quiz-option';
          if (errAnswered) {
            if (oIdx === mp.errorDetection.correctIndex) cls += ' correct';
            else if (oIdx === errChosen) cls += ' wrong';
          }
          return `<button class="${cls}" onclick="answerErrorDet('${q.id}', ${oIdx})">${String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)}. ${escapeHtml(opt)}</button>`;
        }).join('')}
        ${errAnswered ? `
          <div class="box-conclusion" style="margin-top:0.6rem; margin-bottom:0;">
            <strong>Examiner Diagnosis & Correction:</strong> ${escapeHtml(mp.errorDetection.explanation)}
          </div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;">
          <div class="card-title">
            <span>🃏 I. Active Recall Flashcards (Click any card to flip)</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flashcard-grid">
            ${mp.flashcards.map((fc, fIdx) => {
              const fKey = `${q.id}_fc_${fIdx}`;
              const isFlipped = !!state.flippedCards[fKey];
              return `
                <div class="flashcard ${isFlipped ? 'flipped' : ''}" onclick="flipCard('${fKey}')">
                  <div>
                    <span class="badge ${isFlipped ? 'badge-green' : 'badge-blue'}">${escapeHtml(fc.category)} • ${isFlipped ? 'ANSWER' : 'QUESTION'}</span>
                    <p style="font-size:0.9rem; font-weight:600; margin-top:0.55rem;">${escapeHtml(isFlipped ? fc.back : fc.front)}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:0.5rem;">Click to flip ↻</div>
                </div>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;">
          <div class="card-title">
            <span>📅 H. Adjustable Spaced Repetition Checkpoint Scheduler</span>
            <span class="badge badge-amber">Current Interval: ${escapeHtml(state.srSchedule[q.id])}</span>
          </div>
          <p style="font-size:0.86rem; color:var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom:0.65rem;">Select your next review checkpoint for Q${q.number} based on how effortlessly you reconstructed the proof:</p>
          <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.4rem; margin-bottom:0.75rem;">
            ${['Immediate', 'After 1 Day', 'After 3 Days', 'After 7 Days', 'After 14 Days'].map(interval => `
              <button class="btn ${state.srSchedule[q.id] === interval ? 'btn-primary' : ''}" onclick="setSrSchedule('${q.id}', '${interval}')">${interval}</button>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
          <div class="control-group">
            <label>Custom Interval (Days): <strong>${state.srCustomDays[q.id] || 3}d</strong></label>
            <input type="range" min="1" max="30" value="${state.srCustomDays[q.id] || 3}" oninput="setCustomSrDays('${q.id}', this.value)">
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function stepRecallHint(qId, delta) {
  if (delta === 0) state.recallHintLevel[qId] = 0;
  else state.recallHintLevel[qId] = (state.recallHintLevel[qId] || 0) + 1;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function checkFillBlanks(qId) {
  const q = QUESTIONS.find(item => item.id === qId);
  if (!q) return;
  let allCorrect = true;
  q.memoryPalace.fillInBlanks.items.forEach((item, idx) => {
    const sel = document.getElementById(`fill-${qId}-${idx}`);
    if (!sel || sel.value !== item.answer) allCorrect = false;
  });
  const fb = document.getElementById(`fill-feedback-${qId}`);
  if (allCorrect) {
    state.fillBlankStatus[qId] = true;
    saveState();
    if (fb) fb.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--color-conclusion); font-weight:700;">✓ All blanks completed accurately!</span>';
  } else {
    if (fb) fb.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--color-danger); font-weight:700;">✗ One or more blanks are incorrect—check your theorem hypotheses and try again!</span>';
  }
}

function answerErrorDet(qId, optIdx) {
  state.errorDetAnswers[qId] = optIdx;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function flipCard(fKey) {
  state.flippedCards[fKey] = !state.flippedCards[fKey];
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function setSrSchedule(qId, val) {
  state.srSchedule[qId] = val;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function setCustomSrDays(qId, days) {
  state.srCustomDays[qId] = Number(days);
  state.srSchedule[qId] = `Custom: ${days} Days`;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

// --- TAB 7: PRACTICE ARENA (PHASE 8 & PHASE 10) ---
function renderPracticeTab(q) {
  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🏋️ Phase 8: Unseen Variations & Pattern Training Arena — Q${q.number}</span>
        <span class="badge badge-blue">5 Graded Variations (Warm-Up → Exam → Hard → Assumptions → AI/ML)</span>
      </div>
      <p style="font-size:0.88rem; color:var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom:0.9rem;">Attempt each variation first using the progressive hint ladder before revealing the full solution.</p>

      ${q.practice.map((pr, idx) => {
        const key = `${q.id}_pr_${idx}`;
        const hCount = state.practiceHints[key] || 0;
        const isRev = !!state.practiceRevealed[key];
        const grade = state.practiceGrade[key];
        return `
          <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated); margin-bottom:0.95rem;">
            <div class="card-title">
              <span>${escapeHtml(pr.level)}</span>
              ${grade ? `<span class="badge ${grade==='correct'?'badge-green':'badge-amber'}">${grade==='correct'?'Solved Independently ✓':grade==='hints'?'Solved with Hints':'Flagged for Review'}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="math-block" style="background:var(--bg-surface); font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(pr.problem)}</div>
            <div class="box-transform" style="padding:0.55rem 0.85rem; font-size:0.84rem;">
              <strong>🔄 How Changing the Hypothesis Changes the Solution:</strong> ${escapeHtml(pr.hypothesisChange)}
            </div>
            <textarea class="scratchpad" style="min-height:68px;" placeholder="Draft your proof / key inequality for this variation here..."></textarea>
            <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.45rem; margin-top:0.35rem;">
              ${hCount < pr.hints.length ? `<button class="btn" onclick="stepPracticeHint('${key}', ${pr.hints.length})">💡 Hint (${hCount + 1}/${pr.hints.length})</button>` : ''}
              <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="togglePracticeSolution('${key}')">${isRev ? 'Hide Solution' : 'Reveal Full Solution'}</button>
              <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="gradePractice('${key}', 'correct')">✓ Solved Independently</button>
              <button class="btn btn-amber" onclick="gradePractice('${key}', 'review')">⚠️ Review Again</button>
            </div>
            ${hCount > 0 ? `
              <div class="box-assumption" style="margin-top:0.55rem; margin-bottom:0;">
                ${pr.hints.slice(0, hCount).map((h, i) => `<div style="font-size:0.85rem;"><strong>Hint ${i+1}:</strong> ${escapeHtml(h)}</div>`).join('')}
              </div>
            ` : ''}
            ${isRev ? `
              <div class="box-conclusion" style="margin-top:0.6rem; margin-bottom:0;">
                <strong>Rigorous Solution:</strong>
                <p style="font-size:0.89rem; margin-top:0.25rem; white-space:pre-line;">${escapeHtml(pr.solution)}</p>
              </div>
            ` : ''}
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
}

function stepPracticeHint(key, maxH) {
  const cur = state.practiceHints[key] || 0;
  if (cur < maxH) state.practiceHints[key] = cur + 1;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function togglePracticeSolution(key) {
  state.practiceRevealed[key] = !state.practiceRevealed[key];
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function gradePractice(key, val) {
  state.practiceGrade[key] = val;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

// --- VIEW 3: PHASE 11 REVISION CENTER & TIMED MOCK EXAM ---
function renderRevisionView() {
  const totalMarks = GLOBAL_DATA.mockExam.reduce((acc, m) => acc + m.marks, 0);
  let earnedMarks = 0;
  GLOBAL_DATA.mockExam.forEach(m => {
    const perItem = m.marks / m.rubric.length;
    m.rubric.forEach((_, rIdx) => {
      if (state.mockRubric[`${m.id}_${rIdx}`]) earnedMarks += perItem;
    });
  });

  const weakQuestions = QUESTIONS.filter(q => state.mastery[q.id] === 'needs_revision' || state.mastery[q.id] === 'not_started');

  return `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>📜 Phase 11: Integrated Functional Analysis Theorem & Formula Sheet</span>
        <button class="btn btn-amber" onclick="switchGlobalView('last30')">⚡ Launch Last 30 Minutes Mode →</button>
      </div>
      <table class="data-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Theorem / Principle</th>
            <th>Exact Hypotheses (Check Every Single One!)</th>
            <th>Conclusion</th>
            <th>Exam Trigger</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          ${GLOBAL_DATA.theoremSheet.map(t => `
            <tr>
              <td><strong>${escapeHtml(t.name)}</strong></td>
              <td>${escapeHtml(t.hypotheses)}</td>
              <td style="white-space:pre-line;">${escapeHtml(t.conclusion)}</td>
              <td><span class="badge badge-blue">${escapeHtml(t.trigger)}</span></td>
            </tr>
          `).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    <div class="grid-2">
      <div class="card">
        <div class="card-title">🔁 Frequently Used Proof Patterns</div>
        ${GLOBAL_DATA.proofPatterns.map(p => `
          <div class="box-step">
            <div style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(p.pattern)} <span class="badge badge-purple">${escapeHtml(p.usedIn)}</span></div>
            <p style="font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:0.25rem;">${escapeHtml(p.steps)}</p>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <div class="card-title">⚠️ Essential Counterexample Vault</div>
        ${GLOBAL_DATA.counterexamples.map(ce => `
          <div class="box-danger">
            <div style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(ce.name)}: <code>${escapeHtml(ce.object)}</code></div>
            <p style="font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:0.2rem;">${escapeHtml(ce.lesson)}</p>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>🎯 Personalized Revision Queue</span>
        <span class="badge badge-amber">${weakQuestions.length} Questions Queued</span>
      </div>
      ${weakQuestions.length === 0 ? `
        <div class="box-conclusion">🎉 All 6 questions are currently in progress or mastered! Use the Timed Mock Exam below to verify exam readiness.</div>
      ` : `
        <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.5rem;">
          ${weakQuestions.map(wq => `
            <button class="btn" onclick="selectQuestion('${wq.id}', 'memory')">
              Review ${escapeHtml(wq.shortTitle)} (${escapeHtml(state.srSchedule[wq.id])}) →
            </button>
          `).join('')}
        </div>
      `}
    </div>

    <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--accent-primary);">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>⏱️ Timed Mock Examination & Self-Assessment Rubric</span>
        <div style="display:flex; gap:0.45rem; align-items:center;">
          <span class="badge badge-blue" id="mock-timer-display" style="font-size:0.95rem; padding:0.35rem 0.75rem;">${formatTime(state.mockTimerSecs)}</span>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="toggleMockTimer()">${state.mockTimerRunning ? '⏸ Pause Timer' : '▶ Start Timer'}</button>
          <button class="btn" onclick="resetMockTimer(2700)">45m</button>
          <button class="btn" onclick="resetMockTimer(1800)">30m</button>
          <span class="badge badge-green" style="font-size:0.9rem;">Score: ${Math.round(earnedMarks)} / ${totalMarks} Marks</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${GLOBAL_DATA.mockExam.map((m, idx) => `
        <div class="card" style="background:var(--bg-elevated); margin-bottom:0.85rem;">
          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.35rem;">
            <span>Mock Problem ${idx + 1} (Based on ${m.qRef.toUpperCase()})</span>
            <span class="badge badge-amber">${m.marks} Marks • Target: ${m.timeMins} mins</span>
          </div>
          <div class="math-block" style="background:var(--bg-surface);">${escapeHtml(m.prompt)}</div>
          <textarea class="scratchpad" placeholder="Write your timed examination solution here..."></textarea>
          <div style="margin-top:0.45rem;">
            <strong style="font-size:0.84rem;">Self-Grading Examiner Rubric (Check each step you wrote rigorously):</strong>
            ${m.rubric.map((rText, rIdx) => {
              const rKey = `${m.id}_${rIdx}`;
              const checked = !!state.mockRubric[rKey];
              return `
                <label style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:0.3rem; cursor:pointer;">
                  <input type="checkbox" ${checked ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleMockRubric('${rKey}', '${m.qRef}')">
                  <span>${escapeHtml(rText)}</span>
                </label>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
}

function formatTime(secs) {
  const m = Math.floor(secs / 60);
  const s = secs % 60;
  return `${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function toggleMockTimer() {
  state.mockTimerRunning = !state.mockTimerRunning;
  if (state.mockTimerRunning) {
    if (mockTimerInterval) clearInterval(mockTimerInterval);
    mockTimerInterval = setInterval(() => {
      if (state.mockTimerSecs > 0) {
        state.mockTimerSecs--;
        const el = document.getElementById('mock-timer-display');
        if (el) el.textContent = formatTime(state.mockTimerSecs);
      } else {
        state.mockTimerRunning = false;
        clearInterval(mockTimerInterval);
      }
    }, 1000);
  } else {
    if (mockTimerInterval) clearInterval(mockTimerInterval);
  }
  renderApp();
}

function resetMockTimer(secs) {
  state.mockTimerRunning = false;
  if (mockTimerInterval) clearInterval(mockTimerInterval);
  state.mockTimerSecs = secs;
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

function toggleMockRubric(rKey, qRef) {
  state.mockRubric[rKey] = !state.mockRubric[rKey];
  saveState();
  renderApp();
}

// --- VIEW 4: LAST 30 MINUTES BEFORE THE EXAM MODE ---
function renderLast30View() {
  return `
    <div class="card box-transform">
      <div class="card-title">
        <span>⚡ Last 30 Minutes Before the Exam — Rapid-Fire High-Value Trigger Sheet</span>
        <button class="btn" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ Print Rapid-Fire Sheet</button>
      </div>
      <p style="font-size:0.9rem;">Ultra-condensed view containing ONLY the highest-value theorem triggers, 1-line proof ideas, and critical inequalities for Questions 1–6.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="grid-2">
      ${GLOBAL_DATA.last30Minutes.map((item, i) => `
        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid var(--accent-primary);">
          <div style="font-weight:800; color:var(--accent-primary); font-size:1rem; margin-bottom:0.4rem;">${escapeHtml(item.q)}</div>
          <div class="box-transform" style="padding:0.5rem 0.75rem; font-size:0.85rem;">
            <strong>🎯 Instant Proof Trigger:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.trigger)}
          </div>
          <div class="box-step" style="padding:0.5rem 0.75rem; font-size:0.86rem;">
            <strong>💡 1-Line Proof Core:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.oneLine)}
          </div>
          <div class="box-conclusion" style="padding:0.5rem 0.75rem; font-size:0.85rem; margin-bottom:0;">
            <strong>📐 Critical Formula / Inequality:</strong> <code>${escapeHtml(item.criticalInequality)}</code>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
}

// --- PRINT ALL CONTAINER ---
function renderPrintAllContainer() {
  const el = document.getElementById('print-all-container');
  if (!el) return;
  el.innerHTML = `
    <h1>${escapeHtml(GLOBAL_DATA.course)} — ${escapeHtml(GLOBAL_DATA.sheetTitle)}</h1>
    <p>${escapeHtml(GLOBAL_DATA.institution)} • Instructor: ${escapeHtml(GLOBAL_DATA.instructor)} • Date: ${escapeHtml(GLOBAL_DATA.date)}</p>
    <hr style="margin:1rem 0;">
    ${QUESTIONS.map(q => `
      <div class="print-q-block">
        <h2>Question ${q.number}: ${escapeHtml(q.title)}</h2>
        <pre style="white-space:pre-wrap; font-family:serif; background:#f5f5f5; padding:0.5rem;">${escapeHtml(q.originalText)}</pre>
        <h3>Model Examination Solution (Full Proof):</h3>
        <pre style="white-space:pre-wrap; font-family:serif;">${escapeHtml(q.examAnswer.fullProof)}</pre>
      </div>
    `).join('')}
  `;
}


// --- PHASE 4: INTERACTIVE HTML5 CANVAS VISUALIZATION ENGINE ---
let vizAnimId = null;
let vizPlaying = false;
let vizFrame = 0;

const defaultVizParams = {
  q1: { stepN: 8, boundM: 1.5, speed: 1.0, subMode: 'both' },
  q2: { modeK: 5, pertEps: 0.25, speed: 1.0, subMode: 'invert' },
  q3: { caseMode: 1, normL1: 1.4, speed: 1.0 },
  q4: { projT: 1.0, dimD: 4, speed: 1.0 },
  q5: { alpha: 2.0, maxN: 25, speed: 1.0 },
  q6: { decayMode: 'decay', truncN: 6, pVal: 2.0, speed: 1.0 }
};

let vizParams = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultVizParams));

function stopVizAnimation() {
  vizPlaying = false;
  if (vizAnimId) {
    cancelAnimationFrame(vizAnimId);
    vizAnimId = null;
  }
}

function toggleVizPlay() {
  vizPlaying = !vizPlaying;
  const playBtn = document.getElementById('viz-play-btn');
  if (playBtn) playBtn.textContent = vizPlaying ? '⏸ Pause' : '▶ Play';
  if (vizPlaying) runVizLoop();
}

function stepVizForward() {
  vizPlaying = false;
  const playBtn = document.getElementById('viz-play-btn');
  if (playBtn) playBtn.textContent = '▶ Play';
  vizFrame += 18;
  advanceQuestionParamOnStep();
  drawCurrentCanvas();
}

function restartViz() {
  vizFrame = 0;
  const q = getCurrentQuestion();
  if (q.id === 'q1') vizParams.q1.stepN = 1;
  if (q.id === 'q2') vizParams.q2.modeK = 1;
  if (q.id === 'q4') vizParams.q4.projT = 0;
  if (q.id === 'q5') vizParams.q5.maxN = 4;
  if (q.id === 'q6') vizParams.q6.truncN = 1;
  renderVizControls(q);
  drawCurrentCanvas();
}

function resetVizDefaults() {
  stopVizAnimation();
  vizFrame = 0;
  const q = getCurrentQuestion();
  vizParams[q.id] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultVizParams[q.id]));
  renderVizControls(q);
  drawCurrentCanvas();
}

function advanceQuestionParamOnStep() {
  const q = getCurrentQuestion();
  if (q.id === 'q1') {
    vizParams.q1.stepN = (vizParams.q1.stepN % 20) + 1;
  } else if (q.id === 'q2') {
    vizParams.q2.modeK = (vizParams.q2.modeK % 12) + 1;
  } else if (q.id === 'q4') {
    vizParams.q4.projT = Math.min(2.0, Number((vizParams.q4.projT + 0.25).toFixed(2)));
    if (vizParams.q4.projT >= 2.0) vizParams.q4.projT = 0;
  } else if (q.id === 'q5') {
    vizParams.q5.maxN = (vizParams.q5.maxN % 40) + 2;
  } else if (q.id === 'q6') {
    vizParams.q6.truncN = (vizParams.q6.truncN % 16) + 1;
  }
  renderVizControls(q);
}

function runVizLoop() {
  if (!vizPlaying) return;
  const q = getCurrentQuestion();
  const spd = (vizParams[q.id] && vizParams[q.id].speed) || 1.0;
  vizFrame += spd;
  if (Math.floor(vizFrame) % 25 === 0) {
    advanceQuestionParamOnStep();
  }
  drawCurrentCanvas();
  vizAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(runVizLoop);
}

function initOrUpdateCanvas() {
  const q = getCurrentQuestion();
  renderVizControls(q);
  drawCurrentCanvas();
}

function updateVizParam(qId, key, val) {
  if (key === 'decayMode' || key === 'subMode') {
    vizParams[qId][key] = val;
  } else {
    vizParams[qId][key] = Number(val);
  }
  renderVizControls(getCurrentQuestion());
  drawCurrentCanvas();
}

function renderVizControls(q) {
  const bar = document.getElementById('viz-controls-bar');
  if (!bar) return;
  const p = vizParams[q.id];
  let extra = '';

  if (q.id === 'q1') {
    extra = `
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Seq Index n: <strong>${p.stepN}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="1" max="20" step="1" value="${p.stepN}" oninput="updateVizParam('q1','stepN',this.value)">
      </div>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Bound M: <strong>${p.boundM.toFixed(1)}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="0.8" max="2.5" step="0.1" value="${p.boundM}" oninput="updateVizParam('q1','boundM',this.value)">
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (q.id === 'q2') {
    extra = `
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Basis Mode k: <strong>${p.modeK}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="1" max="12" step="1" value="${p.modeK}" oninput="updateVizParam('q2','modeK',this.value)">
      </div>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Perturbation δ: <strong>${p.pertEps.toFixed(2)}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="0.05" max="0.5" step="0.05" value="${p.pertEps}" oninput="updateVizParam('q2','pertEps',this.value)">
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (q.id === 'q3') {
    extra = `
      <button class="btn ${p.caseMode===1?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="updateVizParam('q3','caseMode',1)">Case 1: L₁ Bounded, L₂ Compact</button>
      <button class="btn ${p.caseMode===2?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="updateVizParam('q3','caseMode',2)">Case 2: L₁ Compact, L₂ Continuous</button>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Scale ‖L‖: <strong>${p.normL1.toFixed(1)}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="0.6" max="2.0" step="0.1" value="${p.normL1}" oninput="updateVizParam('q3','normL1',this.value)">
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (q.id === 'q4') {
    extra = `
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Pass Stage (x → Kx → K²x): <strong>${p.projT.toFixed(2)}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="${p.projT}" oninput="updateVizParam('q4','projT',this.value)">
      </div>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Subspace Dim d: <strong>${p.dimD}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="1" max="8" step="1" value="${p.dimD}" oninput="updateVizParam('q4','dimD',this.value)">
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (q.id === 'q5') {
    extra = `
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Exponent α in yₙ = 1/n^α: <strong>${p.alpha.toFixed(2)}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="0.6" max="2.8" step="0.05" value="${p.alpha}" oninput="updateVizParam('q5','alpha',this.value)">
      </div>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Partial Sum Terms N: <strong>${p.maxN}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="3" max="40" step="1" value="${p.maxN}" oninput="updateVizParam('q5','maxN',this.value)">
      </div>
    `;
  } else if (q.id === 'q6') {
    extra = `
      <button class="btn ${p.decayMode==='decay'?'btn-primary':''}" onclick="updateVizParam('q6','decayMode','decay')">λ_k = 1/k → 0 (Compact)</button>
      <button class="btn ${p.decayMode==='nodecay'?'btn-amber':''}" onclick="updateVizParam('q6','decayMode','nodecay')">λ_k ↛ 0 (Non-Compact)</button>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Truncation n: <strong>${p.truncN}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="1" max="16" step="1" value="${p.truncN}" oninput="updateVizParam('q6','truncN',this.value)">
      </div>
      <div class="control-group">
        <label>Norm p: <strong>${p.pVal.toFixed(1)}</strong></label>
        <input type="range" min="1" max="4" step="0.5" value="${p.pVal}" oninput="updateVizParam('q6','pVal',this.value)">
      </div>
    `;
  }

  bar.innerHTML = `
    <button class="btn btn-primary" id="viz-play-btn" onclick="toggleVizPlay()">${vizPlaying ? '⏸ Pause' : '▶ Play'}</button>
    <button class="btn" onclick="stepVizForward()">⏭ Step Forward</button>
    <button class="btn" onclick="restartViz()">⏮ Restart</button>
    ${extra}
    <div class="control-group">
      <label>Speed: <strong>${p.speed.toFixed(1)}x</strong></label>
      <input type="range" min="0.5" max="2.5" step="0.5" value="${p.speed}" oninput="updateVizParam('${q.id}','speed',this.value)">
    </div>
    <button class="btn" onclick="resetVizDefaults()">↺ Reset Default</button>
  `;
}

function drawCurrentCanvas() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('fa-math-canvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const isDark = state.theme === 'dark';
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Background
  ctx.fillStyle = isDark ? '#0f172a' : '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  const q = getCurrentQuestion();
  if (q.id === 'q1') drawQ1Canvas(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, isDark);
  else if (q.id === 'q2') drawQ2Canvas(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, isDark);
  else if (q.id === 'q3') drawQ3Canvas(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, isDark);
  else if (q.id === 'q4') drawQ4Canvas(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, isDark);
  else if (q.id === 'q5') drawQ5Canvas(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, isDark);
  else if (q.id === 'q6') drawQ6Canvas(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, isDark);
}

// --- Q1 CANVAS: CLOSED GRAPH + BOUNDEDNESS VS. UNBOUNDED BIJECTION ---
function drawQ1Canvas(ctx, w, h, isDark) {
  const p = vizParams.q1;
  const n = p.stepN;
  const M = p.boundM;
  const textCol = isDark ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a';
  const mutedCol = isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b';

  // Divider
  ctx.strokeStyle = isDark ? '#334155' : '#cbd5e1';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(w / 2, 15); ctx.lineTo(w / 2, h - 15); ctx.stroke();

  // LEFT PANEL: Part 1 (Bounded f with Closed Graph => Continuous)
  const lCx = w * 0.25, lCy = h * 0.54, scaleX = 95, scaleY = 65;
  // Shaded bounded box [-M, M]
  ctx.fillStyle = isDark ? 'rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.16)' : 'rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.1)';
  ctx.fillRect(30, lCy - M * scaleY, w / 2 - 55, 2 * M * scaleY);
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#059669'; ctx.setLineDash([5, 4]);
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(30, lCy - M * scaleY); ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 25, lCy - M * scaleY);
  ctx.moveTo(30, lCy + M * scaleY); ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 25, lCy + M * scaleY);
  ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);

  // Axes
  ctx.strokeStyle = mutedCol; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(30, lCy); ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 25, lCy);
  ctx.moveTo(lCx, 35); ctx.lineTo(lCx, h - 25);
  ctx.stroke();

  // Curve f(x) = 0.65 * M * sin(1.5x) + 0.25 * M * cos(x)
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2563eb'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let px = -1.8; px <= 1.8; px += 0.04) {
    const fy = 0.65 * M * Math.sin(1.5 * px) + 0.25 * M * Math.cos(px);
    const sx = lCx + px * scaleX;
    const sy = lCy - fy * scaleY;
    if (px === -1.8) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
  }
  ctx.stroke();

  // Sequence x_k = 0.5 + 1.1 / k -> x_0 = 0.5
  const x0 = 0.5;
  const fx0 = 0.65 * M * Math.sin(1.5 * x0) + 0.25 * M * Math.cos(x0);
  for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) {
    const xk = x0 + 1.1 / k;
    const fyk = 0.65 * M * Math.sin(1.5 * xk) + 0.25 * M * Math.cos(xk);
    ctx.fillStyle = k === n ? '#d97706' : 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.45)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(lCx + xk * scaleX, lCy - fyk * scaleY, k === n ? 6 : 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // Target point (x0, f(x0))
  ctx.fillStyle = '#059669';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(lCx + x0 * scaleX, lCy - fx0 * scaleY, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Part 1: Bounded |f(x)| ≤ M + Closed Graph ⇒ Continuous', 32, 26);
  ctx.font = '12px sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = '#059669';
  ctx.fillText(`+M = +${M.toFixed(1)} (Bolzano-Weierstrass Ceiling)`, 36, lCy - M * scaleY - 6);

  // RIGHT PANEL: Part 2 (g(x) = 1/x for x != 0, g(0) = 0)
  const rCx = w * 0.75, rCy = h * 0.54;
  ctx.strokeStyle = mutedCol; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(w / 2 + 25, rCy); ctx.lineTo(w - 25, rCy);
  ctx.moveTo(rCx, 35); ctx.moveTo(rCx, 35); ctx.lineTo(rCx, h - 25);
  ctx.stroke();

  // Hyperbola branches g(x) = 1/x
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#dc2626'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let px = 0.22; px <= 1.85; px += 0.03) {
    const gy = 1 / px;
    const sx = rCx + px * 95;
    const sy = rCy - gy * 34;
    if (px === 0.22) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let px = -1.85; px <= -0.22; px += 0.03) {
    const gy = 1 / px;
    const sx = rCx + px * 95;
    const sy = rCy - gy * 34;
    if (px === -1.85) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
  }
  ctx.stroke();

  // Origin point g(0) = 0
  ctx.fillStyle = '#2563eb';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(rCx, rCy, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillText('g(0) = 0', rCx + 10, rCy + 16);

  // Sequence x_n = 1/n -> 0, g(x_n) = n -> +infinity
  const xn = 1 / n;
  const gn = n;
  const plotY = Math.max(42, rCy - gn * 26);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#d97706';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(rCx + xn * 110, plotY, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Part 2: Bijection g(x)=1/x (x≠0), g(0)=0 (Closed Graph, Discontinuous)', w / 2 + 25, 26);
  ctx.font = '12px sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = '#d97706';
  ctx.fillText(`x_${n} = 1/${n} → 0,  g(x_${n}) = ${n} → +∞ (Escapes ℝ²!)`, w / 2 + 30, 52);

  const tel = document.getElementById('viz-telemetry-bar');
  if (tel) {
    const xkVal = (x0 + 1.1 / n).toFixed(3);
    tel.innerHTML = `<strong>Live Telemetry (n = ${n}):</strong> Left (Part 1): x_${n} = ${xkVal} → 0.500, |f(x_${n})| ≤ ${M.toFixed(1)} trapped in compact [-M, M] ⇒ f(x_${n}) → f(0.5). &nbsp;|&nbsp; Right (Part 2): x_${n} = 1/${n} = ${(1/n).toFixed(3)} → 0, but g(1/${n}) = ${n} shoots to +∞ (no wrong finite limit in ℝ²!).`;
  }
}

// --- Q2 CANVAS: INJECTIVE COMPACT OPERATOR & UNBOUNDED INVERSE ---
function drawQ2Canvas(ctx, w, h, isDark) {
  const p = vizParams.q2;
  const k = p.modeK;
  const textCol = isDark ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a';
  const mutedCol = isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b';

  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Left: Compact Operator K Squashes Unit Modes (Ke_k = e_k / k)', 30, 28);
  ctx.fillText('Right: Inverse Amplification ‖K⁻¹(y_k)‖ / ‖y_k‖ = k → ∞ (Unbounded!)', w / 2 + 20, 28);

  // Left: Mode bars for k = 1..12 showing ||e_k|| = 1 vs ||K e_k|| = 1/k
  const baseX = 45, baseY = h - 45, barW = 24, maxH = 260;
  for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
    const x = baseX + (m - 1) * 32;
    // Unit norm bar (grey outline)
    ctx.strokeStyle = mutedCol;
    ctx.strokeRect(x, baseY - maxH, barW, maxH);
    // Compact image norm 1/m
    const imgH = maxH / m;
    ctx.fillStyle = m === k ? '#d97706' : '#2563eb';
    ctx.fillRect(x, baseY - imgH, barW, imgH);
    ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = '11px sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`e${m}`, x + 3, baseY + 15);
  }

  // Right: Amplification curve of K^-1 on Range(K)
  const rBaseX = w / 2 + 45, rBaseY = h - 45;
  ctx.strokeStyle = mutedCol;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(rBaseX, 50); ctx.lineTo(rBaseX, rBaseY); ctx.lineTo(w - 30, rBaseY);
  ctx.stroke();

  ctx.strokeStyle = '#dc2626'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
    const px = rBaseX + (m - 1) * 32 + 12;
    const py = rBaseY - (m / 12) * 260;
    if (m === 1) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  }
  ctx.stroke();

  for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
    const px = rBaseX + (m - 1) * 32 + 12;
    const py = rBaseY - (m / 12) * 260;
    ctx.fillStyle = m === k ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, m === k ? 7 : 4, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = '#dc2626'; ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(`At mode k = ${k}: ‖Ke_${k}‖ = 1/${k} = ${(1/k).toFixed(3)}, while ‖K⁻¹‖ ≥ ${k} → ∞!`, rBaseX + 15, 70);
  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('If Range(K) were closed ⇒ Bounded Inverse Theorem forces ‖K⁻¹‖ ≤ M < ∞ (Impossible!)', rBaseX + 15, 92);

  const tel = document.getElementById('viz-telemetry-bar');
  if (tel) {
    tel.innerHTML = `<strong>Live Telemetry (Mode k = ${k}):</strong> Input ‖e_${k}‖ = 1.000 &nbsp;|&nbsp; Compact Output ‖Ke_${k}‖ = 1/${k} = ${(1/k).toFixed(4)} &nbsp;|&nbsp; Inverse Stretch Factor ‖K⁻¹(Ke_${k})‖ / ‖Ke_${k}‖ = ${k}.0× (Unbounded as k → ∞ ⇒ Range(K) CANNOT be closed!).`;
  }
}

// --- Q3 CANVAS: 3-SPACE OPERATOR COMPOSITION PIPELINE X -> Y -> Z ---
function drawQ3Canvas(ctx, w, h, isDark) {
  const p = vizParams.q3;
  const isCase1 = p.caseMode === 1;
  const scale = p.normL1;
  const textCol = isDark ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a';
  const cX = w * 0.17, cY = w * 0.5, cZ = w * 0.83, midY = h * 0.55;

  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 14px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Banach Space X', cX - 52, 32);
  ctx.fillText('Banach Space Y', cY - 52, 32);
  ctx.fillText('Banach Space Z', cZ - 52, 32);

  // Arrows X -> Y -> Z
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2563eb'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cX + 85, midY); ctx.lineTo(cY - 85, midY); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cY + 85, midY); ctx.lineTo(cZ - 85, midY); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = '#d97706'; ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(isCase1 ? 'L₁: Bounded' : 'L₁: COMPACT', (cX + cY) / 2 - 42, midY - 12);
  ctx.fillText(isCase1 ? 'L₂: COMPACT' : 'L₂: Continuous', (cY + cZ) / 2 - 45, midY - 12);

  // Space X: Bounded set B (radius 62)
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.15)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2563eb'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cX, midY, 62, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Bounded Set B ⊂ X', cX - 52, midY + 88);

  // Space Y: Image L1(B)
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(cY, midY);
  if (isCase1) {
    // Bounded ball of radius 62 * scale
    const rY = Math.min(82, 52 * scale);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.15)';
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d97706';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, rY, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  } else {
    // Compact thin ellipse Cl(L1(B))
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.2)';
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#059669';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 68, 20, 0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.fillStyle = textCol;
  ctx.fillText(isCase1 ? 'L₁(B) Bounded in Y' : 'K = Cl_Y(L₁(B)) Compact', cY - 68, midY + 88);

  // Space Z: Final Image (L2 o L1)(B) — always precompact!
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(cZ, midY);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.22)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#059669'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 64, 18, -0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  // Draw finite epsilon-net covering balls
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#d97706'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2; ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
  for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(i * 22, -i * 4, 18, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#059669'; ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Cl_Z((L₂∘L₁)(B)) COMPACT!', cZ - 75, midY + 88);

  const tel = document.getElementById('viz-telemetry-bar');
  if (tel) {
    tel.innerHTML = isCase1
      ? `<strong>Case 1 Active (L₁ Bounded, L₂ Compact):</strong> B bounded in X (‖x‖ ≤ R) ⟹ L₁(B) bounded in Y (‖L₁x‖ ≤ ${scale.toFixed(1)}R) ⟹ Compact L₂ crushes L₁(B) into precompact (L₂∘L₁)(B) ⊂ Z.`
      : `<strong>Case 2 Active (L₁ Compact, L₂ Continuous):</strong> B bounded in X ⟹ K = Cl_Y(L₁(B)) is compact in Y ⟹ Continuous L₂ maps K to compact & closed L₂(K) ⊂ Z containing Cl_Z((L₂∘L₁)(B))!`;
  }
}

// --- Q4 CANVAS: COMPACT PROJECTION K = K^2 & RIESZ UNIT BALL ---
function drawQ4Canvas(ctx, w, h, isDark) {
  const p = vizParams.q4;
  const t = p.projT; // 0..1: x -> Kx; 1..2: Kx -> K^2x = Kx
  const d = p.dimD;
  const textCol = isDark ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a';

  // Left: Projection x -> Kx -> K(Kx) = Kx onto Range(K) = Ker(I - K)
  const lCx = w * 0.26, lCy = h * 0.56;
  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Left: Idempotence K(Kx) = Kx ⇒ K|_{Range(K)} = Identity', 28, 28);

  // Subspace line Range(K)
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#059669'; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(40, lCy); ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 30, lCy); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#059669'; ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Y = Range(K) = Ker(I - K) (Closed Subspace)', 52, lCy + 24);

  // Sample points x_i projecting down to Range(K) during t in [0,1], and staying fixed during t in [1,2]!
  const alpha = Math.min(1, t);
  const pts = [[-110, -110], [-45, 95], [35, -125], [105, 85]];
  pts.forEach(([dx, dy]) => {
    const curY = lCy + dy * (1 - alpha);
    const curX = lCx + dx;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.5)'; ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(curX, lCy + dy); ctx.lineTo(curX, lCy); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.fillStyle = t >= 1 ? '#059669' : '#d97706';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(curX, curY, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  });

  // Right: Riesz Packing inside B_{Range(K)}
  const rCx = w * 0.74, rCy = h * 0.55, R = 108;
  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(`Right: Unit Ball B_Y = K(B_Y) Compact ⇒ Finite Dim (d = ${d} < ∞)`, w / 2 + 25, 28);

  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.12)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2563eb'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(rCx, rCy, R, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();

  // Plot d separated unit vectors e_1 .. e_d on unit sphere of Range(K)
  for (let i = 0; i < d; i++) {
    const ang = (i / d) * Math.PI * 2;
    const vx = rCx + R * Math.cos(ang);
    const vy = rCy + R * Math.sin(ang);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d97706'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.8;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(rCx, rCy); ctx.lineTo(vx, vy); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#d97706';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(vx, vy, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = '11px sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`u_${i+1} = Ku_${i+1}`, vx - 22, vy - 10);
  }

  const tel = document.getElementById('viz-telemetry-bar');
  if (tel) {
    const stageStr = t < 1 ? `1st Pass (Applying K: x ↦ Kx, progress ${(t*100).toFixed(0)}%)` : `2nd Pass (Applying K to Kx: K(Kx) = Kx — 100% FIXED on Range(K)!)`;
    tel.innerHTML = `<strong>Live Telemetry:</strong> ${stageStr} &nbsp;|&nbsp; On Y = Range(K), K|_Y = I_Y ⇒ Closed unit ball B_Y = K(B_Y) is compact ⇒ By Riesz's Theorem, dim(Range(K)) = ${d} < ∞!`;
  }
}

// --- Q5 CANVAS: NON-SURJECTIVITY & l_1 MULTIPLIER EXPONENT EXPLORER ---
function drawQ5Canvas(ctx, w, h, isDark) {
  const p = vizParams.q5;
  const alpha = p.alpha;
  const N = p.maxN;
  const textCol = isDark ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a';
  const mutedCol = isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b';

  const padL = 65, padR = 35, padT = 55, padB = 45;
  const plotW = w - padL - padR, plotH = h - padT - padB;

  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(`Partial Sums for y_n = 1/n^α (α = ${alpha.toFixed(2)}): Blue = ∑ |y_n| = ∑ 1/n^α (y ∈ ℓ₁)  vs.  Red = ∑ |x_n| = ∑ n|y_n| = ∑ 1/n^{α-1} (x ∈ ℓ₁)`, padL, 28);

  // Status banner inside canvas
  const inL1 = alpha > 1.0;
  const inRange = alpha > 2.0;
  const isWitness = inL1 && !inRange;
  ctx.fillStyle = isWitness ? '#059669' : '#d97706';
  ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(
    isWitness
      ? `✓ SWEET SPOT α = ${alpha.toFixed(2)} ∈ (1, 2]: ∑ |y_n| < ∞ (y ∈ ℓ₁) AND ∑ n|y_n| = ∞ (y ∉ K(ℓ₁)) ⇒ Valid Witness y ∈ ℓ₁ \\ K(ℓ₁)!`
      : (!inL1 ? `✗ α = ${alpha.toFixed(2)} ≤ 1: Even ∑ |y_n| diverges, so y ∉ ℓ₁!` : `✗ α = ${alpha.toFixed(2)} > 2: Both ∑ |y_n| < ∞ and ∑ n|y_n| < ∞, so y ∈ K(ℓ₁)!`),
    padL, 46
  );

  // Axes
  ctx.strokeStyle = mutedCol; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(padL, padT); ctx.lineTo(padL, padT + plotH); ctx.lineTo(padL + plotW, padT + plotH);
  ctx.stroke();

  // Compute partial sums S1(m) = sum_{k=1}^m 1/k^alpha, S2(m) = sum_{k=1}^m 1/k^{alpha-1}
  let s1 = 0, s2 = 0;
  const pts1 = [], pts2 = [];
  for (let m = 1; m <= N; m++) {
    s1 += Math.pow(m, -alpha);
    s2 += Math.pow(m, -(alpha - 1));
    pts1.push([m, s1]);
    pts2.push([m, s2]);
  }
  const maxY = Math.max(6, Math.min(25, Math.max(s1, s2)));

  // Plot S1 (Blue: ||y||_1)
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2563eb'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.8;
  ctx.beginPath();
  pts1.forEach(([m, val], i) => {
    const px = padL + ((m - 1) / Math.max(1, N - 1)) * plotW;
    const py = padT + plotH - (Math.min(val, maxY) / maxY) * plotH;
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  });
  ctx.stroke();

  // Plot S2 (Red: ||x||_1 = sum n|y_n|)
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#dc2626'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.8;
  ctx.beginPath();
  pts2.forEach(([m, val], i) => {
    const px = padL + ((m - 1) / Math.max(1, N - 1)) * plotW;
    const py = padT + plotH - (Math.min(val, maxY) / maxY) * plotH;
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
  });
  ctx.stroke();

  const tel = document.getElementById('viz-telemetry-bar');
  if (tel) {
    tel.innerHTML = `<strong>Live Telemetry (y_n = 1/n^${alpha.toFixed(2)}, N = ${N}):</strong> Partial Sum ∑_{n=1}^{${N}} |y_n| = <strong>${s1.toFixed(3)}</strong> (${inL1 ? 'Converges ⇒ y ∈ ℓ₁' : 'Diverges ⇒ y ∉ ℓ₁'}) &nbsp;|&nbsp; Preimage Partial Sum ∑_{n=1}^{${N}} n|y_n| = <strong>${s2.toFixed(3)}</strong> (${inRange ? 'Converges ⇒ y ∈ K(ℓ₁)' : 'Diverges to ∞ ⇒ x ∉ ℓ₁, so y ∉ K(ℓ₁)!'}).`;
  }
}

// --- Q6 CANVAS: DIAGONAL OPERATOR SPECTRUM & FINITE-RANK APPROXIMATION ---
function drawQ6Canvas(ctx, w, h, isDark) {
  const p = vizParams.q6;
  const isDecay = p.decayMode === 'decay';
  const truncN = p.truncN;
  const pVal = p.pVal;
  const textCol = isDark ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a';
  const mutedCol = isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b';

  const padL = 55, padB = 45, padT = 50;
  const baseY = h - padB;
  const maxH = h - padT - padB - 10;

  ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = 'bold 13px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(
    isDecay
      ? `Decaying Multipliers λ_k = 1/k → 0: Blue = Finite-Rank L_${truncN} (k ≤ ${truncN}), Amber = Tail L - L_${truncN} (k > ${truncN})`
      : `Non-Decaying Multipliers λ_k = 0.45 + 0.25|cos(k)| ↛ 0: Infinitely Many |λ_{k_ℓ}| > ε = 0.40!`,
    padL, 26
  );

  // Draw 20 multiplier bars
  let tailSup = 0;
  for (let k = 1; k <= 20; k++) {
    const lam = isDecay ? (1 / k) : (0.45 + 0.25 * Math.abs(Math.cos(k)));
    if (k > truncN && lam > tailSup) tailSup = lam;
    const bx = padL + (k - 1) * 39;
    const bh = lam * maxH;
    ctx.fillStyle = k <= truncN ? '#2563eb' : (isDecay ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626');
    ctx.fillRect(bx, baseY - bh, 26, bh);
    ctx.fillStyle = textCol; ctx.font = '11px sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`λ_${k}`, bx + 2, baseY + 15);
  }

  // Horizontal line showing tail operator norm ||L - L_n|| = sup_{k > n} |lambda_k| or epsilon
  const lineVal = isDecay ? tailSup : 0.40;
  const lineY = baseY - lineVal * maxH;
  ctx.strokeStyle = isDecay ? '#059669' : '#dc2626';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.setLineDash([6, 4]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(padL, lineY); ctx.lineTo(w - 30, lineY); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  ctx.fillStyle = isDecay ? '#059669' : '#dc2626';
  ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
  const sepDist = Math.pow(2, 1 / pVal) * 0.40;
  ctx.fillText(
    isDecay
      ? `Tail Operator Norm ‖L - L_${truncN}‖ = sup_{k > ${truncN}} |λ_k| = 1/${truncN + 1} = ${tailSup.toFixed(3)} → 0 as n → ∞ (L IS COMPACT!)`
      : `Threshold ε = 0.40: Pairwise Basis Distance ‖Le_{k_ℓ} - Le_{k_m}‖_${pVal} > 2^{1/${pVal}}·ε = ${sepDist.toFixed(3)} > 0 (NO CAUCHY SUBSEQUENCE!)`,
    padL, 45
  );

  const tel = document.getElementById('viz-telemetry-bar');
  if (tel) {
    tel.innerHTML = isDecay
      ? `<strong>Live Telemetry (Decaying Mode, n = ${truncN}, p = ${pVal}):</strong> dim Range(L_${truncN}) = ${truncN} < ∞ (L_${truncN} is compact) &nbsp;|&nbsp; Operator-Norm Error ‖L - L_${truncN}‖ = sup_{k>${truncN}} |λ_k| = <strong>${tailSup.toFixed(4)}</strong> → 0.`
      : `<strong>Live Telemetry (Non-Decaying Mode, p = ${pVal}):</strong> |λ_{k_ℓ}| > ε = 0.40 for all k_ℓ &nbsp;|&nbsp; Unit basis images satisfy ‖Le_{k_ℓ} - Le_{k_m}‖_${pVal} = (|λ_{k_ℓ}|^p + |λ_{k_m}|^p)^{1/p} > <strong>${sepDist.toFixed(4)}</strong> ∀ ℓ ≠ m.`;
  }
}

// Initialize on window load
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  applyPreferences();
  renderApp();
});

</script>
</body>
